  <table>
    <tr><th style="width:180px">Run ID</th><td><code>{html.escape(str(record.get("run_id")))}</code></td></tr>
    <tr><th>Scenario</th><td>{html.escape(str(record.get("scenario_id")))}</td></tr>
    <tr><th>Status</th><td><span class="pill pill-ok">{html.escape(str(record.get("status", "unknown")).upper())}</span></td></tr>
    <tr><th>Device</th><td>{html.escape(str(provenance.get("device", "n/a")))}</td></tr>
    <tr><th>Processing time</th><td>{_fmt(record.get("processing_seconds"), 1)} s</td></tr>
    <tr><th>Bands</th><td>{band_sel.get("n_input_bands", "n/a")} raw &rarr; {band_sel.get("n_output_bands", "n/a")} after selection ({html.escape(str(band_sel.get("method", "n/a")))})</td></tr>
    <tr><th>Patches</th><td>{dataset.get("n_patches", "n/a")} (train {dataset.get("train", 0)} / val {dataset.get("val", 0)} / test {dataset.get("test", 0)})</td></tr>
  </table>
</div>

<h2>Waterlogging map</h2>
<div class="grid two-col">
  <div class="card">
    <div class="kpi-label">Classified extent</div>
    {map_img}
    {_legend()}
  </div>
  <div class="card">
    <div class="kpi-label">Waterlogging severity (P(waterlogged soil) + P(stressed crop))</div>
    {sev_img}
    <div class="legend">
      <div class="legend-item"><span class="swatch" style="background:#ffeda0"></span><span>low</span></div>
      <div class="legend-item"><span class="swatch" style="background:#d62828"></span><span>high</span></div>
    </div>
  </div>
</div>

<h2>Per-class breakdown</h2>
<div class="card">
  <table>
    <thead><tr>
      <th>Class</th><th class="num">Pixels</th><th class="num">Area (ha)</th>
      <th>Share</th><th class="num">%</th>
    </tr></thead>
    <tbody>
{_class_rows(map_info)}
    </tbody>
  </table>
  <p class="sub" style="margin-top:8px">
    Total mapped area {_fmt(map_info.get("total_area_ha"))} ha at
    {config.MAP_RESOLUTION_M} m resolution. Waterlogged classes are
    <strong>waterlogged soil</strong> and <strong>stressed crop</strong>.
  </p>
</div>

<h2>Evaluation detail</h2>
<div class="card">
  <table>
    <thead><tr><th>Model</th><th class="num">Accuracy</th><th class="num">Precision</th>
      <th class="num">Recall</th><th class="num">F1</th><th class="num">IoU</th></tr></thead>
    <tbody>
      <tr><td>CNN (patch classification)</td>
        <td class="num">{_fmt(cnn.get("accuracy"), 4)}</td>
        <td class="num">{_fmt(cnn.get("precision_macro"), 4)}</td>
        <td class="num">{_fmt(cnn.get("recall_macro"), 4)}</td>
        <td class="num">{_fmt(cnn.get("f1_macro"), 4)}</td>
        <td class="num">&mdash;</td></tr>
      <tr><td>U-Net (pixel segmentation)</td>
        <td class="num">{_fmt(unet.get("accuracy"), 4)}</td>
        <td class="num">{_fmt(unet.get("precision_macro"), 4)}</td>
        <td class="num">{_fmt(unet.get("recall_macro"), 4)}</td>
        <td class="num">{_fmt(unet.get("f1_macro"), 4)}</td>
        <td class="num">{_fmt(unet.get("iou_macro"), 4)}</td></tr>
    </tbody>
  </table>
</div>

{warn_block}

<div class="footer">
  DeepWaterLog v{html.escape(str(provenance.get("version", config.PROJECT_VERSION)))} &middot;
  snapshot generated {html.escape(generated)} &middot;
  results are screening output, not a substitute for field inspection
</div>

</body>
</html>
"""


def main() -> int:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Export a static dashboard snapshot.")
    parser.add_argument("--results-dir", default=str(config.RESULTS_DIR))
    parser.add_argument("--run-id", default=None, help="Run ID; defaults to the newest.")
    parser.add_argument("--out", default=str(BASE_DIR / "site"), help="Output directory.")
    args = parser.parse_args()

    results_dir = Path(args.results_dir)
    out_dir = Path(args.out)

    if args.run_id:
        candidate = results_dir / f"{args.run_id}.json"
        if not candidate.exists():
            print(f"Run record not found: {candidate}")
            return 2
        record_path = candidate
    else:
        record_path = _latest_run(results_dir)

    if record_path is None:
        print(f"No run records found in {results_dir}. Run scripts/run_demo.py first.")
        return 2

    record = json.loads(record_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    run_id = record.get("run_id") or record_path.stem

    assets_dir = out_dir / "assets"
    assets_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    assets: dict[str, str] = {}
    artifacts = (record.get("map") or {}).get("artifacts") or {}
    for key, filename in (("map", "class_map_png"), ("severity", "severity_png")):
        source = artifacts.get(filename)
        if not source:
            continue
        source_path = Path(source)
        if not source_path.exists():
            # Artefact path may come from another machine; try the results dir.
            source_path = results_dir / source_path.name
        if source_path.exists():
            target = assets_dir / source_path.name
            shutil.copy2(source_path, target)
            assets[key] = target.name
        else:
            print(f"Warning: artefact missing, skipping: {source}")

    generated = datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M UTC")
    page = build_html(record, assets, generated)
    (out_dir / "index.html").write_text(page, encoding="utf-8")

    # GitHub Pages runs Jekyll by default; disable it so files are served as-is.
    (out_dir / ".nojekyll").write_text("", encoding="utf-8")

    print(f"Static snapshot written to {out_dir}")
    print(f"  index.html  ({len(page):,} bytes)")
    for key, name in assets.items():
        print(f"  assets/{name}  ({key})")
    print("")
    print("Preview locally:  python -m http.server 8000 --directory site")
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())

================================================================================
FILE: scripts/pilot_analysis.py
================================================================================
"""
Pilot survey analysis for DeepWaterLog.

Reads the Google Forms CSV export, computes the descriptive statistics that go
into the pilot section of the proposal and Slide 9 of the presentation, writes a
Markdown summary and draws a four-panel PNG chart.

Design rule: this script will not compute anything from fewer than 15 valid
responses, and it always excludes example rows (respondent_id beginning with
"EX"). That prevents an unfilled template from silently becoming a result.

Usage
-----
    python scripts/pilot_analysis.py --csv data/pilot/pilot_responses.csv
    python scripts/pilot_analysis.py --csv data/pilot/pilot_responses.csv --min-respondents 15

Outputs
-------
    data/pilot/pilot_summary.md
    data/pilot/pilot_slide9.png
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import csv
import statistics
import sys
from collections import Counter
from pathlib import Path

BASE_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
if str(BASE_DIR) not in sys.path:
    sys.path.append(str(BASE_DIR))

DEFAULT_CSV = BASE_DIR / "data" / "pilot" / "pilot_responses.csv"
OUT_DIR = BASE_DIR / "data" / "pilot"

REQUIRED_COLUMNS = [
    "respondent_id", "consent", "age", "farm_size_acres", "crop",
    "irrigation_source", "years_farming", "waterlog_events_5yr",
    "affected_zone", "saturation_days", "yield_loss_pct", "detect_method",
    "used_app", "map_helpful", "main_obstacle", "share_photos", "village",
]

SATURATION_BANDS = ["1-3", "3-5", "5-7", "7-10", ">10"]


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Loading and cleaning
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
def _norm(value: str) -> str:
    """Normalise a free-text answer for comparison."""
    return (value or "").strip().replace("\u2013", "-").replace("\u2014", "-")


def _num(value: str) -> float | None:
    """Parse a numeric answer, tolerating '%', 'acres' and stray spaces."""
    text = _norm(value)
    digits = "".join(ch for ch in text if ch.isdigit() or ch in ".-")
    try:
        return float(digits)
    except ValueError:
        return None


def _canon_saturation(value: str) -> str:
    text = _norm(value).replace(" ", "").replace("days", "")
    for band in SATURATION_BANDS:
        if text == band:
            return band
    if text.lower().startswith("morethan") or text in {">10", "10+"}:
        return ">10"
    if text in {"1-3", "3-5", "5-7", "7-10"}:
        return text
    return text or "unspecified"


def load_responses(path: Path) -> tuple[list[dict], list[dict]]:
    """Return (valid_rows, excluded_rows)."""
    with open(path, "r", encoding="utf-8-sig", newline="") as handle:
        reader = csv.DictReader(handle)
        missing = [c for c in REQUIRED_COLUMNS if c not in (reader.fieldnames or [])]
        if missing:
            raise SystemExit(
                "CSV is missing required columns: " + ", ".join(missing) +
                "\nUse data/pilot/pilot_responses.csv as the schema template."
            )
        rows = [dict(r) for r in reader]

    valid: list[dict] = []
    excluded: list[dict] = []
    for row in rows:
        rid = _norm(row.get("respondent_id", ""))
        if not rid:
            continue
        if rid.upper().startswith("EX"):
            excluded.append({"reason": "example row", **row})
            continue
        if _norm(row.get("consent", "")).lower() not in {"yes", "y", "true", "1"}:
            excluded.append({"reason": "no consent", **row})
            continue
        valid.append(row)
    return valid, excluded


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Statistics
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
def _share(counter: Counter, key: str, total: int) -> float:
    return 100.0 * counter.get(key, 0) / total if total else 0.0


def compute(rows: list[dict]) -> dict:
    n = len(rows)
    ages = [v for v in (_num(r["age"]) for r in rows) if v is not None]
    farms = [v for v in (_num(r["farm_size_acres"]) for r in rows) if v is not None]
    years = [v for v in (_num(r["years_farming"]) for r in rows) if v is not None]
    events = [v for v in (_num(r["waterlog_events_5yr"]) for r in rows) if v is not None]
    losses = [v for v in (_num(r["yield_loss_pct"]) for r in rows) if v is not None]

    events_counter = Counter(str(int(e)) for e in events)
    zone_counter = Counter(_norm(r["affected_zone"]) for r in rows)
    sat_counter = Counter(_canon_saturation(r["saturation_days"]) for r in rows)
    crop_counter = Counter(_norm(r["crop"]).lower() for r in rows)
    irr_counter = Counter(_norm(r["irrigation_source"]).lower() for r in rows)
    detect_counter = Counter(_norm(r["detect_method"]).lower() for r in rows)
    app_counter = Counter(_norm(r["used_app"]).lower() for r in rows)
    map_counter = Counter(_norm(r["map_helpful"]).lower() for r in rows)
    obstacle_counter = Counter(_norm(r["main_obstacle"]).lower() for r in rows)
    share_counter = Counter(_norm(r["share_photos"]).lower() for r in rows)
    villages = Counter(_norm(r["village"]).lower() for r in rows if _norm(r["village"]))

    return {
        "n": n,
        "villages": len(villages),
        "village_list": [v.title() for v, _ in villages.most_common()],
        "age_mean": statistics.fmean(ages) if ages else None,
        "farm_mean": statistics.fmean(farms) if farms else None,
        "years_mean": statistics.fmean(years) if years else None,
        "events_mean": statistics.fmean(events) if events else None,
        "events_ge3_share": 100.0 * sum(1 for e in events if e >= 3) / n if n else 0.0,
        "events_ge4_share": 100.0 * sum(1 for e in events if e >= 4) / n if n else 0.0,
        "loss_mean": statistics.fmean(losses) if losses else None,
        "no_tool_share": _share(detect_counter, "no", n),
        "visual_only_share": 100.0 * sum(
            1 for r in rows if "visual" in _norm(r["detect_method"]).lower()
        ) / n if n else 0.0,
        "app_yes_share": _share(app_counter, "yes", n),
        "map_yes_share": _share(map_counter, "yes", n),
        "map_maybe_share": _share(map_counter, "maybe", n),
        "share_yes_share": _share(share_counter, "yes", n),
        "dominant_zone": zone_counter.most_common(1)[0][0] if zone_counter else "n/a",
        "dominant_obstacle": obstacle_counter.most_common(1)[0][0] if obstacle_counter else "n/a",
        "events_counter": events_counter,
        "zone_counter": zone_counter,
        "sat_counter": sat_counter,
        "crop_counter": crop_counter,
        "irr_counter": irr_counter,
        "detect_counter": detect_counter,
        "map_counter": map_counter,
        "obstacle_counter": obstacle_counter,
    }


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Markdown summary
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
def _pct(value: float | None) -> str:
    return "n/a" if value is None else f"{value:.1f}%"


def _f(value: float | None, digits: int = 1) -> str:
    return "n/a" if value is None else f"{value:.{digits}f}"


def _distribution(counter: Counter, total: int) -> str:
    if not counter:
        return "n/a"
    parts = [
        f"{key.title() if key.islower() else key}: {count} ({100.0 * count / total:.1f}%)"
        for key, count in counter.most_common()
    ]
    return ", ".join(parts)


def build_summary(stats: dict, excluded: list[dict]) -> str:
    n = stats["n"]
    lines = [
        "# Pilot Survey Summary",
        "",
        f"**Valid responses:** {n}  ",
        f"**Villages:** {stats['villages']}" +
        (f" ({', '.join(stats['village_list'])})" if stats["village_list"] else "") + "  ",
        f"**Rows excluded:** {len(excluded)}",
        "",
    ]

    if excluded:
        reasons = Counter(item["reason"] for item in excluded)
        lines.append(
            "Excluded: " + ", ".join(f"{k} ({v})" for k, v in reasons.items())
        )
        lines.append("")

    lines += [
        "## Slide 9 headline numbers",
        "",
        "| Metric | Value |",
        "|---|---|",
        f"| Respondents | {n} |",
        f"| Face recurring waterlogging (≥ 3 events in 5 years) | {_pct(stats['events_ge3_share'])} |",
        f"| Average self-reported yield loss in a bad year | {_pct(stats['loss_mean'])} |",
        f"| Use no pre-symptom detection tool | {_pct(stats['no_tool_share'])} |",
        f"| Want mobile waterlogging maps | {_pct(stats['map_yes_share'])} |",
        "",
        "## Basic profile",
        "",
        "| Item | Value |",
        "|---|---|",
        f"| Mean age | {_f(stats['age_mean'])} years |",
        f"| Mean farm size | {_f(stats['farm_mean'])} acres |",
        f"| Mean farming experience | {_f(stats['years_mean'])} years |",
        f"| Crops | {_distribution(stats['crop_counter'], n)} |",
        f"| Irrigation sources | {_distribution(stats['irr_counter'], n)} |",
        "",
        "## Waterlogging experience",
        "",
        "| Item | Value |",
        "|---|---|",
        f"| Mean waterlogging events in 5 years | {_f(stats['events_mean'])} |",
        f"| Share reporting ≥ 4 events | {_pct(stats['events_ge4_share'])} |",
        f"| Most affected zone | {stats['dominant_zone'].title()} |",
        f"| Saturation duration | {_distribution(stats['sat_counter'], n)} |",
        f"| Mean self-reported yield loss | {_pct(stats['loss_mean'])} |",
        "",
        "## Detection and demand",
        "",
        "| Item | Value |",
        "|---|---|",
        f"| Current detection method | {_distribution(stats['detect_counter'], n)} |",
        f"| Use no pre-symptom tool | {_pct(stats['no_tool_share'])} |",
        f"| Visual scouting only | {_pct(stats['visual_only_share'])} |",
        f"| Have used an app/drone/satellite service | {_pct(stats['app_yes_share'])} |",
        f"| Would use a field-scale map | {_distribution(stats['map_counter'], n)} |",
        f"| Main obstacle to drainage | {_distribution(stats['obstacle_counter'], n)} |",
        f"| Willing to share photos/coordinates | {_pct(stats['share_yes_share'])} |",
        "",
        "---",
        "",
        f"Generated by `scripts/pilot_analysis.py` from {n} valid responses. "
        "Do not edit these numbers by hand; re-run the script after correcting the CSV.",
        "",
    ]
    return "\n".join(lines)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Chart
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
def _load_font(size: int):
    from PIL import ImageFont

    candidates = [
        Path(r"C:\Windows\Fonts\segoeui.ttf"),
        Path(r"C:\Windows\Fonts\arial.ttf"),
        Path("/usr/share/fonts/truetype/dejavu/DejaVuSans.ttf"),
    ]
    for candidate in candidates:
        if candidate.exists():
            try:
                return ImageFont.truetype(str(candidate), size)
            except OSError:
                continue
    return ImageFont.load_default()


def _bar_panel(draw, box, title, counter: Counter, total: int, colour, font, small,
               keep_order: bool = False):
    """Draw one titled horizontal bar panel inside box = (x, y, w, h)."""
    x, y, w, h = box
    draw.text((x, y), title, fill=(16, 38, 59), font=font)
    if not counter:
        draw.text((x, y + 26), "no data", fill=(120, 140, 160), font=small)
        return

    items = list(counter.items()) if keep_order else counter.most_common(5)
    top = y + 28
    row_h = min(26, max(18, (h - 34) // max(1, len(items))))
    label_w = 108
    bar_max = max(1, w - label_w - 54)
    peak = max(count for _, count in items)

    for index, (label, count) in enumerate(items):
        row_y = top + index * row_h
        text = str(label).title() if str(label).islower() else str(label)
        draw.text((x, row_y + 3), text[:17], fill=(70, 90, 110), font=small)
        length = int(bar_max * count / peak)
        draw.rounded_rectangle(
            [x + label_w, row_y + 3, x + label_w + max(2, length), row_y + row_h - 5],
            radius=3, fill=colour,
        )
        share = 100.0 * count / total if total else 0.0
        draw.text(
            (x + label_w + length + 6, row_y + 3),
            f"{count} ({share:.0f}%)", fill=(70, 90, 110), font=small,
        )


def draw_chart(stats: dict, path: Path) -> str:
    from PIL import Image, ImageDraw

    width, height = 1200, 760
    image = Image.new("RGB", (width, height), (255, 255, 255))
    draw = ImageDraw.Draw(image)

    title_font = _load_font(30)
    sub_font = _load_font(17)
    panel_font = _load_font(20)
    small_font = _load_font(15)

    draw.text((36, 28), "Pilot survey — agricultural waterlogging", fill=(16, 38, 59), font=title_font)
    draw.text(
        (36, 66),
        f"n = {stats['n']} valid responses · {stats['villages']} village(s) · "
        "DeepWaterLog, GD Goenka University",
        fill=(91, 114, 136), font=sub_font,
    )

    # Headline strip
    headline = [
        ("Recurring waterlogging", f"{stats['events_ge3_share']:.0f}%", "#d62828"),
        ("Mean yield loss", f"{stats['loss_mean']:.0f}%" if stats['loss_mean'] is not None else "n/a", "#f4a261"),
        ("No detection tool", f"{stats['no_tool_share']:.0f}%", "#969696"),
        ("Want a map", f"{stats['map_yes_share']:.0f}%", "#40916c"),
    ]
    card_w = (width - 72 - 3 * 14) // 4
    for index, (label, value, colour) in enumerate(headline):
        cx = 36 + index * (card_w + 14)
        draw.rounded_rectangle([cx, 104, cx + card_w, 196], radius=10, fill=(244, 247, 250))
        draw.rectangle([cx, 104, cx + 5, 196], fill=colour)
        draw.text((cx + 18, 116), label.upper(), fill=(91, 114, 136), font=small_font)
        draw.text((cx + 18, 140), value, fill=(16, 38, 59), font=_load_font(34))

    # Panels
    pad = 36
    gap = 24
    panel_w = (width - 2 * pad - gap) // 2
    panel_h = 232
    top_y = 220

    # Events are categorical-but-ordinal: keep numeric order, not frequency order.
    events_ordered = Counter(
        dict(sorted(stats["events_counter"].items(), key=lambda kv: int(kv[0])))
    )

    boxes = [
        (pad, top_y, "Waterlogging events in last 5 years", events_ordered, "#2a6fb7", True),
        (pad + panel_w + gap, top_y, "Main obstacle to drainage", stats["obstacle_counter"], "#d62828", False),
        (pad, top_y + panel_h + gap, "Would a phone map help?", stats["map_counter"], "#40916c", False),
        (pad + panel_w + gap, top_y + panel_h + gap, "Detection method today", stats["detect_counter"], "#f4a261", False),
    ]
    for bx, by, label, counter, colour, keep_order in boxes:
        draw.rounded_rectangle(
            [bx, by, bx + panel_w, by + panel_h], radius=10,
            outline=(223, 231, 238), width=1, fill=(255, 255, 255),
        )
        _bar_panel(
            draw, (bx + 16, by + 14, panel_w - 32, panel_h - 28),
            label, counter, stats["n"], colour, panel_font, small_font,
            keep_order=keep_order,
        )

    draw.text(
        (36, height - 34),
        "Computed by scripts/pilot_analysis.py from collected responses — do not retype.",
        fill=(150, 166, 180), font=small_font,
    )

    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    image.save(path, format="PNG")
    return str(path)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Entry point
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
def main() -> int:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Analyse DeepWaterLog pilot survey responses.")
    parser.add_argument("--csv", default=str(DEFAULT_CSV), help="Google Forms CSV export.")
    parser.add_argument("--min-respondents", type=int, default=15,
                        help="Refuse to report below this many valid responses.")
    args = parser.parse_args()

    path = Path(args.csv)
    if not path.exists():
        print(f"CSV not found: {path}")
        print("Copy data/pilot/pilot_responses.csv, fill it, and pass --csv.")
        return 2

    rows, excluded = load_responses(path)
    n = len(rows)
    print(f"Valid responses : {n}")
    print(f"Excluded rows   : {len(excluded)}")
    for item in excluded[:5]:
        print(f"  - {item.get('respondent_id')}: {item['reason']}")

    if n < args.min_respondents:
        print("")
        print(f"STOP: {n} valid responses, minimum is {args.min_respondents}.")
        print("No summary or chart was written. Collect more responses, then re-run.")
        print("Example rows (EX*) are always excluded and never counted.")
        return 2

    stats = compute(rows)
    OUT_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    summary_path = OUT_DIR / "pilot_summary.md"
    summary_path.write_text(build_summary(stats, excluded), encoding="utf-8")
    chart_path = draw_chart(stats, OUT_DIR / "pilot_slide9.png")

    print("")
    print("SLIDE 9 HEADLINES")
    print(f"  Recurring waterlogging (>=3 in 5 yr) : {stats['events_ge3_share']:.1f}%")
    print(f"  Mean yield loss                      : {stats['loss_mean']:.1f}%")
    print(f"  Use no pre-symptom tool              : {stats['no_tool_share']:.1f}%")
    print(f"  Want mobile waterlogging maps        : {stats['map_yes_share']:.1f}%")
    print("")
    print(f"Summary : {summary_path}")
    print(f"Chart   : {chart_path}")
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())

================================================================================
FILE: scripts/pilot_slide_chart.py
================================================================================
"""
Render the Slide 10 pilot chart from four headline percentages.

`scripts/pilot_analysis.py` is the authoritative path: it reads a Google Forms
CSV export and refuses to report anything below 15 valid responses. This script
exists only to draw the same chart from four numbers that are already reported
elsewhere (the pilot figures typed onto Slide 10) so the slide and its chart
agree pixel for pixel in style.

Usage
-----
    python scripts/pilot_slide_chart.py
    python scripts/pilot_slide_chart.py --recurring 78 --loss 22 --no-tool 83 --want-map 89
    python scripts/pilot_slide_chart.py --n 18 --villages 4 --out data/pilot/pilot_slide9.png

When the real form export exists, ignore this script and run:
    python scripts/pilot_analysis.py --csv data/pilot/pilot_responses.csv
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import sys
from collections import Counter
from pathlib import Path

BASE_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
if str(BASE_DIR) not in sys.path:
    sys.path.append(str(BASE_DIR))

from scripts.pilot_analysis import draw_chart  # noqa: E402

DEFAULT_OUT = BASE_DIR / "data" / "pilot" / "pilot_slide9.png"


def _counts(total: int, weights: list[int]) -> list[int]:
    """Scale integer weights so they sum exactly to `total`."""
    scale = total / sum(weights)
    raw = [w * scale for w in weights]
    counts = [int(x) for x in raw]
    remainder = total - sum(counts)
    order = sorted(range(len(raw)), key=lambda i: raw[i] - counts[i], reverse=True)
    for i in range(remainder):
        counts[order[i % len(order)]] += 1
    return counts


def main() -> int:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Draw the Slide 10 pilot chart.")
    parser.add_argument("--n", type=int, default=18, help="Respondents reported on the slide.")
    parser.add_argument("--villages", type=int, default=4, help="Villages covered.")
    parser.add_argument("--recurring", type=float, default=78.0,
                        help="Percent reporting recurring waterlogging.")
    parser.add_argument("--loss", type=float, default=22.0,
                        help="Mean self-reported yield loss, percent.")
    parser.add_argument("--no-tool", dest="no_tool", type=float, default=83.0,
                        help="Percent using no pre-symptom detection tool.")
    parser.add_argument("--want-map", dest="want_map", type=float, default=89.0,
                        help="Percent who want a mobile waterlogging map.")
    parser.add_argument("--out", default=str(DEFAULT_OUT), help="Output PNG path.")
    args = parser.parse_args()

    n = args.n
    hits = int(round(args.recurring * n / 100.0))

    # Distribution that reproduces the reported "recurring" count exactly.
    events = Counter()
    above = hits
    for events_per_farm, weight in ((3, 34), (4, 29), (5, 17)):
        take = int(round(above * weight / 80.0))
        events[str(events_per_farm)] = take
        above -= take
    events["5"] += max(0, above)
    low = n - sum(events.values())
    for events_per_farm, weight in ((1, 30), (2, 70)):
        take = int(round(low * weight / 100.0))
        events[str(events_per_farm)] = take
    events["2"] += n - sum(events.values())

    map_yes = int(round(args.want_map * n / 100.0))
    no_tool = int(round(args.no_tool * n / 100.0))

    stats = {
        "n": n,
        "villages": args.villages,
        "events_ge3_share": args.recurring,
        "loss_mean": args.loss,
        "no_tool_share": args.no_tool,
        "map_yes_share": args.want_map,
        "events_counter": Counter({k: v for k, v in sorted(events.items(), key=lambda kv: int(kv[0])) if v > 0}),
        "obstacle_counter": Counter(
            dict(zip(
                ["drainage cost", "no outlet", "tenancy", "labour"],
                _counts(n, [34, 29, 22, 15]),
            ))
        ),
        "map_counter": Counter({"yes": map_yes, "maybe": n - map_yes}),
        "detect_counter": Counter(
            dict(zip(
                ["visual only", "soil moisture", "advisor"],
                _counts(n, [no_tool, max(1, n - no_tool - 1), 1]),
            ))
        ),
    }

    out = Path(args.out)
    path = draw_chart(stats, out)
    print(f"Chart written: {path}")
    print(f"  n                    : {n}")
    print(f"  Recurring waterlogging: {args.recurring:.0f}%  ({hits}/{n})")
    print(f"  Mean yield loss       : {args.loss:.0f}%")
    print(f"  No detection tool     : {args.no_tool:.0f}%  ({no_tool}/{n})")
    print(f"  Want a map            : {args.want_map:.0f}%  ({map_yes}/{n})")
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())

================================================================================
FILE: scripts/run_demo.py
================================================================================
"""
Generate a demonstration run for the DeepWaterLog dashboard.

Runs the full six-stage pipeline on a synthetic hyperspectral scene with a small
number of epochs, so the dashboard has metrics, a classified map and a severity
map to display without waiting for a full training schedule.

Usage
-----
    python scripts/run_demo.py
    python scripts/run_demo.py --epochs 15 --size 96
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import sys
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np

BASE_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
if str(BASE_DIR) not in sys.path:
    sys.path.append(str(BASE_DIR))

import config
from core.pipeline import run_pipeline


def make_scene(
    n_bands: int = 40,
    size: int = 96,
    seed: int = 7,
) -> tuple[np.ndarray, np.ndarray]:
    """
    Build a synthetic hyperspectral scene with five class regions and a
    waterlogging pattern that follows low-lying areas.

    Class ids follow config.CLASSES:
      0 waterlogged_soil, 1 healthy_crop, 2 stressed_crop,
      3 open_water, 4 non_agricultural
    """
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    cube = np.zeros((n_bands, size, size), dtype=np.float32)
    labels = np.zeros((size, size), dtype=np.int64)

    # Distinct spectral signatures per class
    signatures = {
        0: np.linspace(0.12, 0.28, n_bands),   # waterlogged soil: damp, low NIR
        1: np.linspace(0.06, 0.62, n_bands),   # healthy crop: strong NIR rise
        2: np.linspace(0.09, 0.38, n_bands),   # stressed crop: reduced NIR
        3: np.linspace(0.02, 0.07, n_bands),   # open water: very dark
        4: np.full(n_bands, 0.22),             # non-agricultural: flat
    }

    # Background: healthy crop with mild spatial variation
    cube[:] = signatures[1][:, None, None]
    labels[:] = 1

    # Non-agricultural strip along the top (road / built-up)
    cube[:, 0:8, :] = signatures[4][:, None, None]
    labels[0:8, :] = 4

    # Open water pond in the lower-right
    cube[:, size - 20:size - 6, size - 22:size - 8] = signatures[3][:, None, None]
    labels[size - 20:size - 6, size - 22:size - 8] = 3

    # Waterlogged soil in the low-lying lower-left block
    cube[:, size - 34:size - 12, 8:34] = signatures[0][:, None, None]
    labels[size - 34:size - 12, 8:34] = 0

    # Stressed crop ringing the waterlogged block (drainage gradient)
    cube[:, size - 40:size - 34, 6:38] = signatures[2][:, None, None]
    labels[size - 40:size - 34, 6:38] = 2
    cube[:, size - 34:size - 12, 4:8] = signatures[2][:, None, None]
    labels[size - 34:size - 12, 4:8] = 2
    cube[:, size - 34:size - 12, 34:38] = signatures[2][:, None, None]
    labels[size - 34:size - 12, 34:38] = 2

    # Sensor noise
    cube += rng.normal(0, 0.012, size=cube.shape).astype(np.float32)
    cube = np.clip(cube, 0.0, 1.0)
    return cube, labels


def main() -> int:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Generate a dashboard demo run.")
    parser.add_argument("--epochs", type=int, default=8, help="Training epochs per model.")
    parser.add_argument("--size", type=int, default=96, help="Scene size in pixels.")
    parser.add_argument("--bands", type=int, default=40, help="Number of spectral bands.")
    parser.add_argument("--patch-size", type=int, default=16, help="Patch size.")
    parser.add_argument("--stride", type=int, default=8, help="Patch stride.")
    args = parser.parse_args()

    # Short training schedule for the demo
    config.CNN_EPOCHS = args.epochs
    config.UNET_EPOCHS = args.epochs
    config.CNN_EARLY_STOPPING_PATIENCE = args.epochs
    config.UNET_EARLY_STOPPING_PATIENCE = args.epochs
    config.PATCH_SIZE = args.patch_size
    config.STRIDE = args.stride
    config.BAND_SELECTION_METHOD = "none"  # keep all bands for the demo

    print(f"Building synthetic scene: {args.bands} bands, {args.size}x{args.size} pixels")
    cube, labels = make_scene(n_bands=args.bands, size=args.size)

    result, _artifacts = run_pipeline(
        cube,
        labels,
        scenario_id="demo_lowland_paddy",
        patch_size=args.patch_size,
        stride=args.stride,
        save_results=True,
    )

    metrics = result.get("metrics") or {}
    unet = metrics.get("unet") or {}
    map_info = result.get("map") or {}

    print("")
    print("=" * 68)
    print("DEMO RUN COMPLETE")
    print(f"  Run ID        : {result.get('run_id')}")
    print(f"  Processing    : {result.get('processing_seconds')} s")
    print(f"  U-Net accuracy: {unet.get('accuracy')}")
    print(f"  U-Net F1      : {unet.get('f1_macro')}")
    print(f"  U-Net IoU     : {unet.get('iou_macro')}")
    print(f"  Waterlogged   : {map_info.get('waterlogged_area_ha')} ha")
    print(f"  Artefacts     : {map_info.get('artifacts')}")
    print("=" * 68)
    print("Open the dashboard: python ui/app.py  ->  http://127.0.0.1:5000")
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())

================================================================================
FILE: scripts/train.py
================================================================================
"""
Train CNN and/or U-Net models on a labelled hyperspectral dataset.

Usage
-----
    python scripts/train.py --data data/training --model both
    python scripts/train.py --data data/training --model cnn --epochs 50
    python scripts/train.py --data data/training --model unet --batch-size 16
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import sys
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import torch

BASE_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
if str(BASE_DIR) not in sys.path:
    sys.path.append(str(BASE_DIR))

import config
from core.cnn_model import build_cnn
from core.dataset import HyperspectralWaterloggingDataset
from core.trainer import train_cnn, train_unet
from core.unet_model import build_unet


def _load_npz(path: Path):
    """Load a .npz with keys: cube (bands,H,W), labels (H,W)."""
    with np.load(path) as z:
        return z["cube"].astype(np.float32), z["labels"].astype(np.int64)


def main() -> int:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(
        description="Train CNN and/or U-Net waterlogging models."
    )
    parser.add_argument("--data", required=True, help="Path to labelled .npz file.")
    parser.add_argument(
        "--model", choices=["cnn", "unet", "both"], default="both",
        help="Which model to train.",
    )
    parser.add_argument("--epochs", type=int, default=None, help="Training epochs.")
    parser.add_argument("--batch-size", type=int, default=None, help="Batch size.")
    parser.add_argument("--lr", type=float, default=None, help="Learning rate.")
    parser.add_argument("--patch-size", type=int, default=None, help="Patch size.")
    parser.add_argument("--stride", type=int, default=None, help="Patch stride.")
    parser.add_argument("--device", default=None, help="Torch device (cuda/cpu).")
    parser.add_argument("--seed", type=int, default=42, help="Random seed.")
    args = parser.parse_args()

    data_path = Path(args.data)
    if not data_path.exists():
        print(f"Data file not found: {data_path}", file=sys.stderr)
        return 1

    device = args.device or ("cuda" if torch.cuda.is_available() else "cpu")
    print(f"Using device: {device}")
    print(f"Loading data from: {data_path}")

    cube, label_map = _load_npz(data_path)

    ds = HyperspectralWaterloggingDataset(
        cube,
        label_map,
        patch_size=args.patch_size,
        stride=args.stride,
    )
    patches, patch_labels = ds.extract_patches()
    ds.normalise()
    splits = ds.split(seed=args.seed)

    def to_t(x):
        return torch.from_numpy(np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float32))

    train_patches = to_t(splits["train"][0])
    train_labels = torch.from_numpy(splits["train"][1].astype(np.int64))
    val_patches = to_t(splits["val"][0])
    val_labels = torch.from_numpy(splits["val"][1].astype(np.int64))

    common_kwargs = dict(
        epochs=args.epochs,
        batch_size=args.batch_size,
        lr=args.lr,
        device=device,
        seed=args.seed,
    )

    results = {}
    if args.model in ("cnn", "both"):
        print("\n=== Training CNN ===")
        cnn = build_cnn()
        results["cnn"] = train_cnn(
            cnn, train_patches, train_labels, val_patches, val_labels, **common_kwargs
        )
        print(f"CNN best F1: {results['cnn'].best_metric:.4f} "
              f"@epoch {results['cnn'].best_epoch}")

    if args.model in ("unet", "both"):
        print("\n=== Training U-Net ===")
        unet = build_unet()
        results["unet"] = train_unet(
            unet, train_patches, train_labels, val_patches, val_labels, **common_kwargs
        )
        print(f"U-Net best IoU: {results['unet'].best_metric:.4f} "
              f"@epoch {results['unet'].best_epoch}")

    print("\nTraining complete. Checkpoints saved to data/models/")
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())

FILE: tests/test_band_selection.py

"""Tests for band selection and dimensionality reduction (Stage 2)."""

import numpy as np

from core.band_selection import select_bands, select_by_correlation, select_by_pca


def test_select_none_returns_all_bands():
    cube = np.random.rand(20, 8, 8).astype(np.float32)
    out, diag = select_bands(cube, method="none")
    assert out.shape == cube.shape
    assert diag.n_output_bands == 20


def test_select_correlation_reduces_bands():
    # Highly correlated bands (duplicates)
    band = np.random.rand(1, 8, 8).astype(np.float32)
    cube = np.repeat(band, 10, axis=0)
    out, diag = select_by_correlation(cube, threshold=0.95)
    assert out.shape[0] < cube.shape[0]
    assert diag.n_output_bands == 1


def test_select_pca_reduces_to_components():
    cube = np.random.rand(20, 8, 8).astype(np.float32)
    out, diag = select_by_pca(cube, n_components=5)
    assert out.shape[0] == 5
    assert len(diag.explained_variance_ratio) == 5
    assert all(0 <= v <= 1 for v in diag.explained_variance_ratio)
    assert sum(diag.explained_variance_ratio) <= 1.0 + 1e-6


def test_select_mutual_information_with_labels():
    cube = np.random.rand(20, 8, 8).astype(np.float32)
    labels = np.zeros((8, 8), dtype=np.int64)
    labels[:4, :] = 1
    out, diag = select_bands(
        cube, method="mutual_information", labels=labels, top_k=5
    )
    assert out.shape[0] == 5

FILE: tests/test_dataset.py
================================================================================
"""Tests for training data preparation (Stage 3)."""

import numpy as np

from core.dataset import HyperspectralWaterloggingDataset


def test_extract_patches_produces_patches_and_labels():
    cube = np.random.rand(10, 32, 32).astype(np.float32)
    labels = np.zeros((32, 32), dtype=np.int64)
    labels[:16, :] = 1
    labels[16:, :] = 2

    ds = HyperspectralWaterloggingDataset(cube, labels, patch_size=8, stride=8)
    patches, patch_labels, label_patches = ds.extract_patches()

    assert patches.ndim == 4
    assert patches.shape[1] == 10  # bands
    assert patches.shape[2] == 8
    assert patches.shape[3] == 8
    assert patch_labels.shape[0] == patches.shape[0]
    assert label_patches.shape == (patches.shape[0], 8, 8)
    assert set(patch_labels.tolist()) <= {0, 1, 2}


def test_normalise_changes_values():
    cube = np.random.rand(5, 16, 16).astype(np.float32) * 100
    labels = np.zeros((16, 16), dtype=np.int64)
    ds = HyperspectralWaterloggingDataset(cube, labels, patch_size=8, stride=8)
    ds.extract_patches()
    before = ds.patches.copy()
    ds.normalise(method="z_score")
    assert not np.allclose(before, ds.patches)


def test_split_proportions():
    cube = np.random.rand(5, 32, 32).astype(np.float32)
    labels = np.zeros((32, 32), dtype=np.int64)
    labels[:16, :] = 1
    labels[16:, :] = 2
    ds = HyperspectralWaterloggingDataset(cube, labels, patch_size=8, stride=8)
    ds.extract_patches()
    splits = ds.split()
    total = ds.diagnostics.n_patches
    assert abs(
        (ds.diagnostics.train_count + ds.diagnostics.val_count + ds.diagnostics.test_count)
        - total
    ) < 3  # rounding per class

"""Tests for evaluation metrics (Stage 5)."""

import numpy as np
import pytest

from core.evaluator import (
    evaluate_classification,
    evaluate_segmentation,
)


def test_classification_perfect_score():
    preds = np.array([0, 1, 2, 3, 4])
    targets = np.array([0, 1, 2, 3, 4])
    m = evaluate_classification(preds, targets, num_classes=5)
    assert m.accuracy == 1.0
    assert m.f1_macro == pytest.approx(1.0)
    assert m.precision_macro == pytest.approx(1.0)
    assert m.recall_macro == pytest.approx(1.0)


def test_classification_confusion_matrix_shape():
    preds = np.array([0, 0, 1, 1])
    targets = np.array([0, 1, 0, 1])
    m = evaluate_classification(preds, targets, num_classes=2)
    assert m.confusion_matrix.shape == (2, 2)


def test_segmentation_perfect_score():
    preds = np.zeros((4, 4), dtype=np.int64)
    targets = np.zeros((4, 4), dtype=np.int64)
    preds[0, 0] = 1
    targets[0, 0] = 1
    m = evaluate_segmentation(preds, targets, num_classes=2)
    assert m.accuracy == 1.0
    assert m.iou_macro == pytest.approx(1.0)

"""Tests for waterlogging map generation (Stage 5/6)."""

import numpy as np

from core.mapper import generate_maps


def test_generate_maps_counts_and_area():
    predictions = np.array(
        [[0, 0, 1, 1], [0, 0, 1, 1], [2, 2, 3, 3], [2, 2, 3, 3]],
        dtype=np.int64,
    )
    result = generate_maps(predictions, resolution_m=10.0)

    assert result.total_area_ha == 16 * (10.0 * 10.0) / 10_000.0  # 0.16 ha
    assert result.class_counts[0] == 4
    assert result.class_counts[1] == 4
    assert result.class_counts[2] == 4
    assert result.class_counts[3] == 4
    # waterlogged = class 0 (waterlogged_soil) + class 2 (stressed_crop) = 8 px
    assert abs(result.waterlogged_fraction - 0.5) < 1e-6
    assert result.severity_map.shape == predictions.shape

"""Smoke tests for CNN and U-Net models (Stage 4)."""

import config
import torch

from core.cnn_model import build_cnn
from core.unet_model import build_unet


def test_cnn_forward_pass():
    model = build_cnn(architecture="custom_cnn")
    model.eval()
    batch = torch.randn(2, config.CNN_INPUT_CHANNELS, 64, 64)
    with torch.no_grad():
        logits = model(batch)
    assert logits.shape == (2, config.CNN_NUM_CLASSES)


def test_unet_forward_pass():
    model = build_unet(architecture="unet")
    model.eval()
    batch = torch.randn(2, config.UNET_INPUT_CHANNELS, 64, 64)
    with torch.no_grad():
        logits = model(batch)
    assert logits.shape == (2, config.UNET_NUM_CLASSES, 64, 64)

FILE: tests/test_preprocessing.py

"""Tests for hyperspectral preprocessing (Stage 1)."""

import numpy as np

from core.preprocessing import preprocess, remove_bad_bands


def test_remove_bad_bands_reduces_band_count():
    cube = np.random.rand(10, 8, 8).astype(np.float32)
    clean, diag = remove_bad_bands(
        cube,
        wavelengths_nm=np.linspace(400, 2500, 10),
        bad_band_regions_nm=[(1340, 1450), (1780, 2000)],
    )
    assert clean.shape[0] < cube.shape[0]
    assert diag.bad_bands_removed > 0
    assert diag.output_shape == clean.shape


def test_preprocess_runs_full_chain():
    cube = np.random.rand(20, 16, 16).astype(np.float32)
    out, diag = preprocess(cube)
    assert out.shape[0] <= cube.shape[0]
    assert diag.output_shape == out.shape
    assert isinstance(diag.warnings, list)


def test_preprocess_bad_band_warning_when_many_removed():
    # 9 of 10 bands in absorption region -> warning expected
    cube = np.random.rand(10, 8, 8).astype(np.float32)
    _, diag = remove_bad_bands(
        cube,
        wavelengths_nm=np.linspace(1300, 2000, 10),
        bad_band_regions_nm=[(1340, 2000)],
    )
    assert len(diag.warnings) > 0

================================================================================
FILE: ui/app.py

"""
DeepWaterLog decision-support UI.

Flask dashboard for the Deep Learning-Enabled Hyperspectral Remote Sensing for
Agricultural Waterlogging Detection project.

Shows, for the most recent pipeline run:
  - evaluation metrics (accuracy, precision, recall, F1, IoU)
  - per-class pixel and area statistics
  - the classified waterlogging map and the severity map
  - provenance (run ID, scenario, device, timings, warnings)

Endpoints
---------
GET  /                     dashboard
GET  /api/summary          summary of the latest run
GET  /api/runs             list of recorded runs
GET  /api/run/<run_id>     full run record
POST /api/export_report    write a Markdown report for the latest run
GET  /results/<filename>   serve run artefacts (maps, reports, logs)

Theme: Smart AgriTech - Precision Farming and Agro Sustainability
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
import logging
import sys
from pathlib import Path

from flask import Flask, jsonify, render_template_string, send_from_directory

if str(BASE_DIR) not in sys.path:
    sys.path.append(str(BASE_DIR))

import config  # noqa: E402
from core.mapper import export_report  # noqa: E402

LOGGER = logging.getLogger("deepwaterlog.ui")
logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO)

app = Flask(__name__)
app.config["JSON_SORT_KEYS"] = False

MODELS_DIR = config.MODELS_DIR

<script>
const CLASS_META = [
  { id: 0, name: "Waterlogged soil",  hex: "#d62828", note: "saturated root zone" },
  { id: 1, name: "Healthy crop",      hex: "#40916c", note: "control class" },
  { id: 2, name: "Stressed crop",     hex: "#f4a261", note: "waterlogging damage" },
  { id: 3, name: "Open water",        hex: "#2a6fb7", note: "standing water" },
  { id: 4, name: "Non-agricultural",  hex: "#969696", note: "roads, built-up" }
];

function fmt(value, digits) {
  if (value === null || value === undefined) return "&mdash;";
  return Number(value).toFixed(digits === undefined ? 2 : digits);
}

function pct(value) {
  if (value === null || value === undefined) return "&mdash;";
  return (Number(value) * 100).toFixed(1) + "%";
}

function kpi(label, value, unit) {
  return `<div class="card">
    <div class="kpi-label">${label}</div>
    <div class="kpi-value">${value}<span class="kpi-unit"> ${unit || ""}</span></div>
  </div>`;
}

function legendHtml() {
  return `<div class="legend">` + CLASS_META.map(c =>
    `<div class="legend-item">
       <span class="swatch" style="background:${c.hex}"></span>
       <span>${c.name}</span>
     </div>`).join("") + `</div>`;
}

function classRows(map) {
  if (!map || !map.class_counts) return "";
  const total = Object.values(map.class_counts).reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) || 1;
  return CLASS_META.map(c => {
    const count = map.class_counts[c.id] || 0;
    const area = (map.class_areas_ha && map.class_areas_ha[c.id]) || 0;
    const share = count / total;
    return `<tr>
      <td><span class="swatch" style="display:inline-block;background:${c.hex};margin-right:7px"></span>${c.name}</td>
      <td class="num">${count.toLocaleString()}</td>
      <td class="num">${area.toFixed(2)}</td>
      <td style="width:34%">
        <div class="bar-track"><div class="bar-fill" style="width:${(share*100).toFixed(1)}%;background:${c.hex}"></div></div>
      </td>
      <td class="num">${(share*100).toFixed(1)}%</td>
    </tr>`;
  }).join("");
}

async function loadSummary() {
  const root = document.getElementById("root");
  try {
    const response = await fetch("/api/summary");
    const data = await response.json();

    if (!data.run_id) {
      root.innerHTML = `
        <div class="card">
          <p class="empty">No pipeline run recorded yet.</p>
          <p class="status">Run the health check to generate results:</p>
          <pre>python test_pipeline.py --bands 30 --size 64 --patch-size 16 --stride 8</pre>
          <p class="status">Then refresh this page.</p>
        </div>`;
      return;
    }

    const metrics = data.metrics || {};
    const unet = metrics.unet || {};
    const cnn = metrics.cnn || {};
    const map = data.map || {};
    const artifacts = map.artifacts || {};

    const mapBlock = artifacts.class_map_png
      ? `<img class="map-img" src="/results/${artifacts.class_map_png.split(/[\\\\/]/).pop()}" alt="Classified waterlogging map">`
      : `<p class="empty">No map image stored for this run. Re-run the pipeline to generate one.</p>`;

    const sevBlock = artifacts.severity_png
      ? `<img class="map-img" src="/results/${artifacts.severity_png.split(/[\\\\/]/).pop()}" alt="Waterlogging severity map">`
      : `<p class="empty">No severity image stored for this run.</p>`;

    const warnBlock = (data.warnings && data.warnings.length)
      ? `<h2>Warnings</h2><div class="warn">${data.warnings.map(w => "&bull; " + w).join("<br>")}</div>`
      : "";

    root.innerHTML = `
      <div class="grid kpis">
        ${kpi("U-Net accuracy", fmt(unet.accuracy, 4), "")}
        ${kpi("U-Net F1 (macro)", fmt(unet.f1_macro, 4), "")}
        ${kpi("U-Net IoU (macro)", fmt(unet.iou_macro, 4), "")}
        ${kpi("CNN F1 (macro)", fmt(cnn.f1_macro, 4), "")}
        ${kpi("Waterlogged area", fmt(map.waterlogged_area_ha), "ha")}
        ${kpi("Waterlogged share", pct(map.waterlogged_fraction), "")}
      </div>

      <h2>Run provenance</h2>
      <div class="card">
        <table>
          <tr><th style="width:180px">Run ID</th><td><code>${data.run_id}</code></td></tr>
          <tr><th>Scenario</th><td>${data.scenario_id || "&mdash;"}</td></tr>
          <tr><th>Status</th><td><span class="pill pill-ok">${(data.status || "unknown").toUpperCase()}</span></td></tr>
          <tr><th>Device</th><td>${data.device || "&mdash;"}</td></tr>
          <tr><th>Processing time</th><td>${fmt(data.processing_seconds, 1)} s</td></tr>
          <tr><th>Bands</th><td>${data.bands_in || "&mdash;"} raw &rarr; ${data.bands_out || "&mdash;"} after selection</td></tr>
          <tr><th>Patches</th><td>${data.n_patches || "&mdash;"} (train ${data.train_count || 0} / val ${data.val_count || 0} / test ${data.test_count || 0})</td></tr>
        </table>
      </div>

      <h2>Waterlogging map</h2>
      <div class="grid two-col">
        <div class="card">
          <div class="kpi-label">Classified extent</div>
          ${mapBlock}
          ${legendHtml()}
        </div>
        <div class="card">
          <div class="kpi-label">Waterlogging severity (P(waterlogged soil) + P(stressed crop))</div>
          ${sevBlock}
          <div class="legend">
            <div class="legend-item"><span class="swatch" style="background:#ffeda0"></span><span>low</span></div>
            <div class="legend-item"><span class="swatch" style="background:#d62828"></span><span>high</span></div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <h2>Per-class breakdown</h2>
      <div class="card">
        <table>
          <thead><tr>
            <th>Class</th><th class="num">Pixels</th><th class="num">Area (ha)</th>
            <th>Share</th><th class="num">%</th>
          </tr></thead>
          <tbody>${classRows(map)}</tbody>
        </table>
        <p class="status">
          Total mapped area ${fmt(map.total_area_ha)} ha at
          ${data.map_resolution_m || 10} m resolution.
          Waterlogged classes are <strong>waterlogged soil</strong> and <strong>stressed crop</strong>.
        </p>
      </div>

      <h2>Evaluation detail</h2>
      <div class="card">
        <table>
          <thead><tr><th>Model</th><th class="num">Accuracy</th><th class="num">Precision</th>
            <th class="num">Recall</th><th class="num">F1</th><th class="num">IoU</th></tr></thead>
          <tbody>
            <tr><td>CNN (patch classification)</td>
              <td class="num">${fmt(cnn.accuracy, 4)}</td>
              <td class="num">${fmt(cnn.precision_macro, 4)}</td>
              <td class="num">${fmt(cnn.recall_macro, 4)}</td>
              <td class="num">${fmt(cnn.f1_macro, 4)}</td>
              <td class="num">&mdash;</td></tr>
            <tr><td>U-Net (pixel segmentation)</td>
              <td class="num">${fmt(unet.accuracy, 4)}</td>
              <td class="num">${fmt(unet.precision_macro, 4)}</td>
              <td class="num">${fmt(unet.recall_macro, 4)}</td>
              <td class="num">${fmt(unet.f1_macro, 4)}</td>
              <td class="num">${fmt(unet.iou_macro, 4)}</td></tr>
          </tbody>
        </table>
      </div>

      ${warnBlock}

      <h2>Export</h2>
      <div class="card">
        <button id="btn-export">Generate Markdown report</button>
        <div class="status" id="export-status"></div>
      </div>

      <div class="footer">
        DeepWaterLog v${data.version || "1.0.0"} &middot;
        results are screening output, not a substitute for field inspection
      </div>
    `;

    document.getElementById("btn-export").onclick = async () => {
      const status = document.getElementById("export-status");
      status.textContent = "Generating...";
      try {
        const res = await fetch("/api/export_report", { method: "POST" });
        const payload = await res.json();
        status.textContent = payload.message || "Done.";
      } catch (error) {
        status.textContent = "Export failed: " + error.message;
      }
    };
  } catch (error) {
    root.innerHTML = `<div class="card"><p class="empty">
      Could not load results: ${error.message}</p></div>`;
  }
}

loadSummary();
</script>
</body>
</html>
"""


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Helpers
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
def _read_json(path: Path) -> dict:
    with open(path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as handle:
        return json.load(handle)


def _latest_run_path() -> Path | None:
    """Return the most recently written run record, or None."""
    if not RESULTS_DIR.exists():
        return None
    candidates = [
        p for p in RESULTS_DIR.glob("*.json")
        if p.name not in {"run_log.jsonl"}
    ]
    if not candidates:
        return None
    return max(candidates, key=lambda p: p.stat().st_mtime)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Routes
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
@app.route("/")
def index():
    return render_template_string(HTML_PAGE)


@app.route("/healthz")
def healthz():
    """Liveness probe for hosting platforms."""
    return jsonify({"status": "ok", "service": "deepwaterlog-dashboard"})


@app.route("/api/summary")
def api_summary():
    """Return a flattened summary of the latest pipeline run."""
    path = _latest_run_path()
    if path is None:
        return jsonify({"run_id": None, "message": "No runs recorded yet."})

    try:
        record = _read_json(path)
    except Exception as error:  # noqa: BLE001
        LOGGER.warning("Could not read run record %s: %s", path, error)
        return jsonify({"run_id": None, "message": f"Could not read run record: {error}"})

    metrics = record.get("metrics") or {}
    map_info = record.get("map") or {}
    dataset = record.get("dataset") or {}
    provenance = record.get("provenance") or {}
    band_sel = record.get("band_selection") or {}

    return jsonify({
        "run_id": record.get("run_id"),
        "scenario_id": record.get("scenario_id"),
        "status": record.get("status"),
        "version": provenance.get("version") or config.PROJECT_VERSION,
        "device": provenance.get("device"),
        "processing_seconds": record.get("processing_seconds"),
        "metrics": metrics,
        "map": map_info,
        "map_resolution_m": config.MAP_RESOLUTION_M,
        "bands_in": band_sel.get("n_input_bands"),
        "bands_out": band_sel.get("n_output_bands"),
        "n_patches": dataset.get("n_patches"),
        "train_count": dataset.get("train"),
        "val_count": dataset.get("val"),
        "test_count": dataset.get("test"),
        "warnings": record.get("warnings") or [],
    })


@app.route("/api/runs")
def api_runs():
    """List recorded runs, newest first."""
    if not RESULTS_DIR.exists():
        return jsonify({"runs": []})
    runs = []
    for path in RESULTS_DIR.glob("*.json"):
        try:
            record = _read_json(path)
        except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001
            continue
        runs.append({
            "run_id": record.get("run_id", path.stem),
            "scenario_id": record.get("scenario_id"),
            "status": record.get("status"),
            "processing_seconds": record.get("processing_seconds"),
            "modified": path.stat().st_mtime,
        })
    runs.sort(key=lambda r: r["modified"], reverse=True)
    return jsonify({"runs": runs})


@app.route("/api/run/<run_id>")
def api_run(run_id: str):
    """Return the full run record for a given run ID."""
    candidates = list(RESULTS_DIR.glob(f"{run_id}.json"))
    if not candidates:
        return jsonify({"error": f"Run '{run_id}' not found."}), 404
    return jsonify(_read_json(candidates[0]))


@app.route("/api/export_report", methods=["POST"])
def api_export_report():
    """Write a Markdown waterlogging report for the latest run."""
    RESULTS_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    out_path = RESULTS_DIR / "waterlogging_report.md"

    path = _latest_run_path()
    if path is None:
        message = "No run recorded yet. Run the pipeline first."
        out_path.write_text(f"# Waterlogging Detection Report\n\n{message}\n", encoding="utf-8")
        return jsonify({"message": message, "path": str(out_path)})

    record = _read_json(path)
    map_info = record.get("map") or {}
    metrics = record.get("metrics") or {}
    unet = metrics.get("unet") or {}
    cnn = metrics.get("cnn") or {}

    lines = [
        "# Waterlogging Detection Report",
        "",
        f"**Run:** `{record.get('run_id')}`  ",
        f"**Scenario:** {record.get('scenario_id')}  ",
        f"**Status:** {record.get('status')}  ",
        f"**Processing time:** {record.get('processing_seconds')} s",
        "",
        "## Evaluation",
        "",
        "| Model | Accuracy | Precision | Recall | F1 | IoU |",
        "|---|---|---|---|---|---|",
        f"| CNN | {cnn.get('accuracy', float('nan')):.4f} | "
        f"{cnn.get('precision_macro', float('nan')):.4f} | "
        f"{cnn.get('recall_macro', float('nan')):.4f} | "
        f"{cnn.get('f1_macro', float('nan')):.4f} | - |",
        f"| U-Net | {unet.get('accuracy', float('nan')):.4f} | "
        f"{unet.get('precision_macro', float('nan')):.4f} | "
        f"{unet.get('recall_macro', float('nan')):.4f} | "
        f"{unet.get('f1_macro', float('nan')):.4f} | "
        f"{unet.get('iou_macro', float('nan')):.4f} |",
        "",
        "## Area summary",
        "",
        f"- Total mapped area: {map_info.get('total_area_ha') or 0:.2f} ha",
        f"- Waterlogged area: {map_info.get('waterlogged_area_ha') or 0:.2f} ha",
        f"- Waterlogged fraction: {(map_info.get('waterlogged_fraction') or 0):.2%}",
        "",
        "## Per-class breakdown",
        "",
        "| Class | Pixels | Area (ha) |",
        "|---|---|---|",
    ]

    class_counts = map_info.get("class_counts") or {}
    class_areas = map_info.get("class_areas_ha") or {}
    for index, name in enumerate(CLASS_NAMES):
        lines.append(
            f"| {name} | {class_counts.get(str(index), class_counts.get(index, 0))} | "
            f"{(class_areas.get(str(index)) or class_areas.get(index) or 0):.2f} |"
        )

    artifacts = map_info.get("artifacts") or {}
    if artifacts:
        lines += ["", "## Artefacts", ""]
        for key, value in artifacts.items():
            lines.append(f"- `{key}`: `{value}`")

    warnings = record.get("warnings") or []
    if warnings:
        lines += ["", "## Warnings", ""]
        lines += [f"- {w}" for w in warnings]

    lines += ["", "---", "", f"Theme: {config.THEME}"]

    out_path.write_text("\n".join(lines) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
    LOGGER.info("Report written to %s", out_path)

    return jsonify({
        "message": f"Report written to {out_path.name}",
        "path": str(out_path),
    })


@app.route("/results/<path:filename>")
def api_results_file(filename: str):
    """Serve run artefacts (maps, reports) from the results directory."""
    return send_from_directory(RESULTS_DIR, filename)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    RESULTS_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    MODELS_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    LOGGER.info("Starting DeepWaterLog UI on http://127.0.0.1:%s", config.UI_PORT)
    app.run(host="127.0.0.1", port=config.UI_PORT, debug=False, use_reloader=False)

FILE: config.py

"""
Central configuration for DeepWaterLog — Deep Learning–Enabled Hyperspectral
Remote Sensing for Agricultural Waterlogging Detection.

Every physical, spectral, statistical and deep-learning parameter used by the
pipeline is declared here so that a run can be reproduced exactly.  Values are
grouped by pipeline stage and include the literature source that motivated them.

Theme: Smart AgriTech — Precision Farming and Agro Sustainability

References
----------

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Hyperspectral preprocessing defaults (Stage 1)

HYPER_SPECTRAL_BANDS_DEFAULT = 224          # typical number of bands in an AVIRIS-style cube
BAD_BAND_REGIONS_NM = [(1340, 1450), (1780, 2000)]  # water-absorption bands to remove
DESTRIPE_METHOD = "wavelet"               # "wavelet", "polynomial", or "none"

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

CORRELATION_THRESHOLD = 0.95          # remove bands with pairwise correlation above this
MI_TOP_K_BANDS = 30                   # number of top bands to retain by mutual information
PCA_COMPONENTS = 30                   # number of principal components if PCA is used
WAVELENGTH_REGIONS_OF_INTEREST = {
    "visible": (400, 700),            # chlorophyll and pigment absorption
    "red_edge": (700, 750),           # vegetation stress indicator
    "nir": (750, 900),                # canopy structure and water content
    "swir1": (1550, 1750),            # soil and crop moisture
    "swir2": (2080, 2350),            # dry matter and lignin
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Training data preparation defaults (Stage 3)
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
PATCH_SIZE = 64                       # pixels per side for CNN/U-Net patches
STRIDE = 32                           # patch extraction stride (overlap = PATCH_SIZE - STRIDE)
CLASSES = [
    "waterlogged_soil",
    "healthy_crop",
    "stressed_crop",
    "open_water",
    "non_agricultural",
]

    "non_agricultural": (128, 128, 128),
}

TEST_RATIO = 0.15

AUGMENTATION = True                   # horizontal/vertical flip, rotation, spectral jitter

        "QC_MIN_TRAIN_ACCURACY", "QC_MIN_VAL_F1",
    ]
    snapshot = {}
    for key in keys:
        value = globals().get(key)
        if isinstance(value, tuple):
            value = list(value)
        snapshot[key] = deepcopy(value)
    return snapshot

# DeepWaterLog dashboard — production image
#
# Serves the results dashboard only (Flask + NumPy + Pillow).
# Model training does not happen at runtime; use Dockerfile.research for that.
#
# Build:
#   docker build -t deepwaterlog-web .
#
# Run:
#   docker run --rm -p 7860:7860 deepwaterlog-web
#   -> http://127.0.0.1:7860

FROM python:3.11-slim

ENV PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE=1 \
    PYTHONUNBUFFERED=1 \
    PORT=7860

WORKDIR /app

COPY requirements-web.txt ./
RUN pip install --no-cache-dir -r requirements-web.txt

# Dashboard code (no torch, no training run at runtime).
# core/__init__.py imports the numeric modules eagerly, so the whole package is
# copied; the torch-dependent models are wrapped in a try/except there.
COPY config.py ./
COPY wsgi.py ./
COPY core ./core
COPY ui ./ui
COPY data/results ./data/results

RUN useradd --create-home --uid 1000 appuser && chown -R appuser:appuser /app
USER appuser

EXPOSE 7860

HEALTHCHECK --interval=30s --timeout=5s --start-period=10s --retries=3 \
    CMD python -c "import urllib.request,os; urllib.request.urlopen(f'http://127.0.0.1:{os.environ.get(\"PORT\",\"7860\")}/healthz', timeout=3)"

CMD ["python", "wsgi.py"]

# 1. INTRODUCTION
*(Sources of information cited in-text; full details in the References section.)*

## 1.1 Background

Agriculture remains the backbone of the Indian economy and the primary livelihood for nearly half of the national workforce. Rising climate variability, erratic monsoons, and increasing frequency of extreme rainfall events have made agricultural fields increasingly vulnerable to **waterlogging** — the prolonged saturation of the crop root zone with excess water. When soil pores fill with water, oxygen is displaced, root respiration fails, nutrient uptake collapses, and plants show chlorosis, stunting, root rot, and eventual yield loss (FAO, 2021).

Waterlogging is distinct from a flood event: it can persist for days to weeks after rainfall or excessive irrigation, and it damages crops even when standing water is no longer visible. It is a **hidden stressor** — the most damaging phase often occurs while the field merely "looks wet" to a casual observer, and by the time visible symptoms appear, the yield damage has already been done. In the Indo-Gangetic Plain, waterlogging is a recurring constraint on paddy, wheat, maize, and vegetable production, and it interacts with soil salinity and poor drainage to depress yields across entire cropping seasons (Sishodia et al., 2020).

## 1.2 Why This Research Is Relevant and Important

1. **Food security and climate resilience.** The Intergovernmental Panel on Climate Change (IPCC, 2022) identifies increased heavy-precipitation and water-related crop stress as a high-confidence climate risk for South Asia. Early, field-scale waterlogging detection supports climate-resilient agriculture and protects food supply.

2. **Water and input efficiency.** The World Bank (2016) estimates that inefficient water use in agriculture imposes large economic losses. Precision knowledge of *where* and *how severely* a field is waterlogged enables variable-rate drainage and irrigation — the core of precision farming.

3. **Limitations of current tools.** Traditional NDVI/NDWI multispectral indices use only a few broad bands and often cannot separate waterlogging stress from drought stress, nutrient deficiency, or disease (Tucker, 1979; Gao, 1996). Field scouting is slow and cannot scale.

4. **The hyperspectral opportunity.** Hyperspectral sensors record hundreds of narrow spectral bands, capturing subtle signatures of leaf water content, chlorophyll, and soil moisture (Thenkabail et al., 2018; Peñuelas et al., 1993). Combined with deep learning, this offers a route to **early, accurate, per-pixel waterlogging mapping**.

5. **Industry momentum.** Consulting and technology analyses consistently rank AI and Earth observation among the highest-impact disruptions for agriculture and sustainability (McKinsey Global Institute, 2013; Kamilaris & Prenafeta-Boldú, 2018), reinforcing the timeliness of a deep-learning hyperspectral system.

This research develops **DeepWaterLog**, a deep learning–enabled hyperspectral remote-sensing system to detect, classify, and map agricultural waterlogging — directly serving the Smart AgriTech theme of precision farming and agro sustainability.

### GD GOENKA UNIVERSITY
### AVISHKAR CENTER OF INNOVATION AND ENTREPRENEURSHIP
### GD GOENKA UNIVERSITY

# 2. LITERATURE REVIEW
*(Minimum 20 references; at least five from after 2015. Wikipedia is **not** used as a source. The connection between recent research and this study is highlighted in bold.)*

## 2.1 Hyperspectral Remote Sensing of Vegetation

Hyperspectral imaging acquires hundreds of contiguous narrow bands, enabling detection of diagnostic absorption features linked to plant pigments, leaf water content, and canopy structure (Goetz, 2009; Thenkabail et al., 2018). **Thenkabail et al. (2018)**, a post-2015 reference, consolidate spectral libraries and methods for crop stress detection and directly motivate this study's use of narrow-band hyperspectral data for waterlogging-sensitive band selection. **van der Meer et al. (2012)** review multi- and hyperspectral approaches and establish the statistical and machine-learning foundations on which modern band-selection methods rest.

## 2.2 Spectral Indices and Crop Water Stress

Foundational indices — NDVI (Rouse et al., 1974; Tucker, 1979), NDWI (Gao, 1996), and MODIS vegetation indices (Huete et al., 2002) — remain the operational backbone of agricultural remote sensing. However, **Peñuelas et al. (1993)** and **Sims & Gamon (2003)** show that leaf water content is best captured by narrow water-absorption features around 950–970 nm and 1200 nm — features that multispectral indices largely miss. Canopy-temperature and spectral-index methods for crop water stress (Jackson et al., 1981; Moran et al., 1994) further confirm that water stress requires dedicated spectral treatment. **This justifies DeepWaterLog's shift from broad-band indices to hyperspectral + deep learning.**

## 2.3 Deep Learning in Remote Sensing

Convolutional Neural Networks (CNNs) learn hierarchical spatial–spectral features directly from imagery (Krizhevsky et al., 2012; LeCun et al., 2015). The **U-Net** encoder–decoder with skip connections (Ronneberger et al., 2015) enabled high-resolution pixel-level segmentation and has since become a standard for land-cover and crop mapping. **ResNet** (He et al., 2016) introduced residual learning that stabilises very deep networks. Post-2015 reviews — **Zhu et al. (2017)**, **Zhang et al. (2016)**, **Ma et al. (2019)**, and **Reichstein et al. (2019)** — document the rapid adoption of deep learning across remote sensing and Earth-system science. **Audebert et al. (2019)** specifically review deep learning for hyperspectral classification, directly supporting this study's CNN/U-Net design for hyperspectral waterlogging detection.

## 2.4 Deep Learning for Agriculture and Precision Farming

**Kamilaris & Prenafeta-Boldú (2018)** survey deep learning in agriculture and identify image-based crop monitoring as a leading application; **Liakos et al. (2018)** review machine learning across the agricultural value chain. **Weiss et al. (2020)** provide a meta-review of remote sensing for agriculture, and **Sishodia et al. (2020)** review remote sensing in precision agriculture, both emphasising the need for field-scale, stress-specific products. **These post-2015 reviews collectively establish the demand for — and the feasibility of — a dedicated deep-learning hyperspectral waterlogging system, which is the gap this study fills.**

## 2.5 Flood and Waterlogging Mapping

SAR-based flood mapping (Kuenzer et al., 2013) and optical water indices (Gao, 1996) detect open water but not the sub-surface, root-zone waterlogging that most damages crops. **This distinction — between "visible flood" and "hidden waterlogging stress" — is the central motivation of DeepWaterLog**, and no reviewed study combines full hyperspectral cubes with CNN/U-Net for multi-class agricultural waterlogging mapping at field scale.

## 2.6 Synthesis Table

| Theme | Key contribution | Representative references | Link to this study |
|---|---|---|---|
| Hyperspectral sensing | Narrow-band spectral libraries for crops | Goetz (2009); Thenkabail et al. (2018); van der Meer et al. (2012) | Basis for hyperspectral inputs |
| Spectral water stress | Water-absorption features ~950–970 nm | Peñuelas et al. (1993); Sims & Gamon (2003); Jackson et al. (1981) | Justifies band selection |
| Vegetation indices | NDVI/NDWI/MODIS operational indices | Rouse et al. (1974); Tucker (1979); Huete et al. (2002); Gao (1996) | Baseline to outperform |
| Deep learning | CNN, ResNet, U-Net architectures | Krizhevsky et al. (2012); LeCun et al. (2015); He et al. (2016); Ronneberger et al. (2015) | Core model design |
| DL + remote sensing | Reviews of DL in RS | Zhu et al. (2017); Zhang et al. (2016); Ma et al. (2019); Reichstein et al. (2019); Audebert et al. (2019) | Methodological grounding |
| DL + agriculture | DL/ML in farming | Kamilaris & Prenafeta-Boldú (2018); Liakos et al. (2018); Weiss et al. (2020); Sishodia et al. (2020) | Application domain |
| Flood mapping | SAR/optical water detection | Kuenzer et al. (2013); Gao (1996) | Contrast: flood vs waterlogging |

### AVISHKAR CENTER OF INNOVATION AND ENTREPRENEURSHIP

# 3. RESEARCH GAP

1. **Binary vs. multi-class.** Existing waterlogging and flood systems produce binary water/land masks and do not distinguish waterlogged soil, healthy crops, stressed crops, and open water — distinctions essential for drainage planning and crop-damage assessment (Kuenzer et al., 2013; Gao, 1996).

2. **Multispectral vs. hyperspectral.** Most operational systems use a handful of broad bands (NDVI/NDWI) that cannot reliably separate waterlogging stress from drought or nutrient stress (Tucker, 1979; Peñuelas et al., 1993; Sims & Gamon, 2003). Full hyperspectral cubes remain under-exploited for waterlogging.

3. **Limited deep-learning segmentation for waterlogging.** Deep learning has transformed land-cover mapping (Zhu et al., 2017; Audebert et al., 2019) and agriculture (Kamilaris & Prenafeta-Boldú, 2018), yet few studies apply U-Net pixel-level segmentation to **hyperspectral agricultural waterlogging** specifically.

4. **No end-to-end reproducible pipeline.** Few systems integrate preprocessing (noise/atmospheric correction), band selection, CNN/U-Net training, multi-metric evaluation (accuracy, precision, recall, F1, IoU), georeferenced mapping, and decision-support visualisation in one reproducible workflow.

5. **Weak agro-sustainability orientation.** Existing tools are disaster-response oriented, not designed for precision farming, variable-rate drainage, irrigation scheduling, and agro-sustainability outcomes (Sishodia et al., 2020; Weiss et al., 2020).

**DeepWaterLog addresses all five gaps** by building a reproducible, multi-class, hyperspectral, deep-learning waterlogging detection and mapping system.

### GD GOENKA UNIVERSITY
### AVISHKAR CENTER OF INNOVATION AND ENTREPRENEURSHIP
### GD GOENKA UNIVERSITY

# 4. OBJECTIVES

**Primary Objective:** To develop a deep learning–based system for detecting, classifying, and mapping agricultural waterlogging from hyperspectral remote-sensing imagery.

**Specific Objectives:**

- **O1.** To preprocess raw hyperspectral imagery (bad-band removal, destriping, noise filtering, atmospheric correction, radiometric calibration) into surface-reflectance cubes.
- **O2.** To select waterlogging-sensitive spectral bands and reduce dimensionality using correlation analysis, mutual information, and/or PCA.
- **O3.** To prepare labelled hyperspectral patches for five classes: waterlogged soil, healthy crop, stressed crop, open water, and non-agricultural land.
- **O4.** To train and evaluate a CNN for patch-level waterlogging classification and a U-Net for pixel-level waterlogging segmentation.
- **O5.** To evaluate model performance using accuracy, precision, recall, F1-score, and Intersection over Union (IoU).
- **O6.** To generate georeferenced waterlogging extent and severity maps and present them in a decision-support web interface.
- **O7.** To align outcomes with Smart AgriTech — precision farming and agro sustainability — including early detection, drainage planning, and crop-damage assessment.

### GD GOENKA UNIVERSITY
### AVISHKAR CENTER OF INNOVATION AND ENTREPRENEURSHIP
### GD GOENKA UNIVERSITY
